Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a algebras algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 917. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 551/1.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the algebras space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 917 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 551. QED.

Theorem 221.5 (Spectral Property)

Statement: Let the algebras structure S satisfy the conditions of spectral property framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 15. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 350 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 15 is immediate from the spectral property property. For the inductive step, we decompose the algebras structure using the spectral property theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 350. Each subproblem has a solution by the spectral property fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
